70: type PostgresDispatchRepository struct {
71: 	db *pgxpool.Pool
72: }
73: 
74: func NewDispatchRepository(db *pgxpool.Pool) DispatchRepository {
75: 	return &PostgresDispatchRepository{db: db}
76: }
77: 
78: // ExecuteAssignment atomically links a shipment to a trip and records an audit
79: // entry. Both operations run in a single transaction so the audit trail and
80: // shipment link stay consistent.
81: func (r *PostgresDispatchRepository) ExecuteAssignment(ctx context.Context, tx *AssignmentTx) (*AssignmentResult, error) {
82: 	if tx == nil {
83: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("assignment transaction is nil")
84: 	}
85: 
86: 	actionType := tx.ActionType
87: 	if actionType == "" {
88: 		actionType = ActionManualAssign
89: 	}
90: 
91: 	const updateShipment = `
92: 		UPDATE customer_v2_shipments
93: 		   SET trip_id = $1, status = 'ASSIGNED', updated_at = NOW()
94: 		 WHERE id = $2 AND tenant_id = $3`
95: 	const insertAction = `
96: 		INSERT INTO dispatch_actions
97: 		    (tenant_id, action_type, shipment_id, trip_id, vehicle_id, driver_id, performed_at)
98: 		VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, NOW())`
99: 
100: 	dbTx, err := r.db.BeginTx(ctx, pgx.TxOptions{})
101: 	if err != nil {
102: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to begin transaction: %w", err)
103: 	}
104: 	defer func() { _ = dbTx.Rollback(ctx) }()
105: 
106: 	if _, err := dbTx.Exec(ctx, updateShipment, tx.TripID, tx.ShipmentID, tx.TenantID); err != nil {
107: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to link shipment to trip: %w", err)
108: 	}
109: 
110: 	if _, err := dbTx.Exec(ctx, insertAction,
111: 		tx.TenantID, actionType, tx.ShipmentID, tx.TripID, tx.VehicleID, tx.DriverID,
112: 	); err != nil {
113: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to insert dispatch action: %w", err)
114: 	}
115: 
116: 	if err := dbTx.Commit(ctx); err != nil {
117: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to commit transaction: %w", err)
118: 	}
119: 
120: 	return &AssignmentResult{
121: 		TripID:         tx.TripID,
122: 		Status:         "SCHEDULED",
123: 		ShipmentStatus: "ASSIGNED",
124: 	}, nil
125: }
126: 
127: // SaveSuggestion persists a ranked dispatch suggestion for auditing and
128: // historical analysis.
129: func (r *PostgresDispatchRepository) SaveSuggestion(ctx context.Context, s *DispatchSuggestion) error {
130: 	reasonsJSON, _ := json.Marshal(s.Reasons)
131: 	const query = `
132: 		INSERT INTO dispatch_suggestions
133: 		    (tenant_id, shipment_id, suggestion_rank, vehicle_id, driver_id, score, distance_km, eta_min, reasons)
134: 		VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7, $8, $9)`
135: 
136: 	startTime := time.Now()
137: 	_, err := r.db.Exec(ctx, query,
138: 		s.TenantID, s.ShipmentID, s.Rank, s.VehicleID, s.DriverID,
139: 		s.Score, s.DistanceKm, s.EtaMin, reasonsJSON,
140: 	)
141: 	_ = startTime // query timing placeholder
142: 	return err
143: }
144: 
145: // GetActiveTripsForVehicle returns the IDs of trips that are not in a terminal
146: // state for the given vehicle (used for availability checks).
147: func (r *PostgresDispatchRepository) GetActiveTripsForVehicle(ctx context.Context, vehicleID uuid.UUID) ([]uuid.UUID, error) {
148: 	const query = `
149: 		SELECT id FROM trips
150: 		 WHERE vehicle_id = $1
151: 		   AND status IN ('CREATED', 'STARTED', 'PAUSED', 'RESUMED')
152: 		   AND is_deleted = FALSE`
153: 
154: 	rows, err := r.db.Query(ctx, query, vehicleID)
155: 	if err != nil {
156: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to query active trips: %w", err)
157: 	}
158: 	defer rows.Close()
159: 
160: 	var tripIDs []uuid.UUID
161: 	for rows.Next() {
162: 		var id uuid.UUID
163: 		if err := rows.Scan(&id); err != nil {
164: 			return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to scan trip id: %w", err)
165: 		}
166: 		tripIDs = append(tripIDs, id)
167: 	}
168: 	return tripIDs, rows.Err()
169: }

106: 		logger:       logger,
107: 	}
108: }
109: 
110: // ─── Assign ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
111: 
112: // Assign creates a real trip from a confirmed shipment and links them.
113: // It validates shipment status, checks vehicle availability, creates the trip
114: // via TripService, updates the shipment link, records an audit entry, and
115: // publishes domain events.
116: func (s *dispatchService) Assign(ctx context.Context, tenantID uuid.UUID, req AssignRequest) (*AssignResult, error) {
117: 	// 1. Fetch the shipment
118: 	shipment, err := s.customerRepo.GetShipmentByID(ctx, req.ShipmentID)
119: 	if err != nil {
120: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to fetch shipment: %w", err)
121: 	}
122: 	if shipment == nil {
123: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("shipment %s not found", req.ShipmentID)
124: 	}
125: 
126: 	// 2. Validate shipment status — must be CONFIRMED
127: 	if shipment.Status != "CONFIRMED" {
128: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("shipment must be in CONFIRMED status, got: %s", shipment.Status)
129: 	}
130: 
131: 	// 3. Validate shipment has no existing trip
132: 	if shipment.TripID != nil && *shipment.TripID != uuid.Nil {
133: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("shipment already assigned to trip %s", shipment.TripID)
134: 	}
135: 
136: 	// 4. Check vehicle availability (no active trips)
137: 	if s.dispatchRepo != nil {
138: 		activeTrips, err := s.dispatchRepo.GetActiveTripsForVehicle(ctx, req.VehicleID)
139: 		if err != nil {
140: 			s.logger.Warn("failed to check vehicle availability, proceeding anyway", zap.Error(err))
141: 		} else if len(activeTrips) > 0 {
142: 			return nil, fmt.Errorf("vehicle %s has %d active trips, cannot assign", req.VehicleID, len(activeTrips))
143: 		}
144: 	}
145: 
146: 	// 5. Build CreateTripRequest from shipment data, then inject selected vehicle/driver
147: 	tripReq, err := buildTripRequestFromShipment(shipment)
148: 	if err != nil {
149: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to build trip request: %w", err)
150: 	}
151: 	tripReq.VehicleID = req.VehicleID.String()
152: 	tripReq.DriverID = req.DriverID.String()
153: 
154: 	// 6. Create the trip via TripService
155: 	trip, err := s.tripSvc.Create(ctx, tenantID.String(), tripReq)
156: 	if err != nil {
157: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to create trip: %w", err)
158: 	}
159: 
160: 	// 7. Link shipment to trip
161: 	tripUUID, err := uuid.Parse(trip.ID)
162: 	if err != nil {
163: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid trip ID returned: %w", err)
164: 	}
165: 	shipment.TripID = &tripUUID
166: 	shipment.Status = "ASSIGNED"
167: 	if err := s.customerRepo.UpdateShipment(ctx, shipment); err != nil {
168: 		s.logger.Error("failed to link shipment to trip",
169: 			zap.String("trip_id", trip.ID),
170: 			zap.String("shipment_id", req.ShipmentID.String()),
171: 			zap.Error(err))
172: 		// Don't fail the assignment — the trip is created, just log the link failure
173: 	}
174: 
175: 	// 8. Audit: record dispatch action
176: 	if s.dispatchRepo != nil {
177: 		assignmentTx := &dispatchRepo.AssignmentTx{
178: 			ShipmentID: req.ShipmentID,
179: 			TripID:     trip.ID,
180: 			VehicleID:  req.VehicleID,
181: 			DriverID:   req.DriverID,
182: 			TenantID:   tenantID,
183: 			ActionType: dispatchRepo.ActionManualAssign,
184: 		}
185: 		if _, err := s.dispatchRepo.ExecuteAssignment(ctx, assignmentTx); err != nil {
186: 			s.logger.Warn("failed to record dispatch action", zap.Error(err))
187: 		}
188: 	}
189: 
190: 	// 9. Publish events (best-effort)
191: 	s.publishEvent("trip.assigned", map[string]interface{}{
192: 		"trip_id":     trip.ID,
193: 		"shipment_id": req.ShipmentID.String(),
194: 		"vehicle_id":  req.VehicleID.String(),
195: 		"driver_id":   req.DriverID.String(),
196: 		"tenant_id":   tenantID.String(),
197: 	})
198: 	s.publishEvent("shipment.assigned", map[string]interface{}{
199: 		"shipment_id": req.ShipmentID.String(),
200: 		"trip_id":     trip.ID,
201: 		"tenant_id":   tenantID.String(),
202: 	})
203: 
204: 	s.logger.Info("shipment dispatched to vehicle",
205: 		zap.String("trip_id", trip.ID),
206: 		zap.String("shipment_id", req.ShipmentID.String()),
207: 		zap.String("vehicle_id", req.VehicleID.String()),
208: 	)
209: 
210: 	return &AssignResult{
211: 		TripID:         trip.ID,
212: 		Status:         "SCHEDULED",
213: 		ShipmentStatus: "ASSIGNED",
214: 	}, nil
215: }
216: 
217: // ─── Redispatch ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
218: 
219: // Redispatch fails the existing trip (if in CREATED or SCHEDULED) and creates
220: // a new one with a different vehicle/driver, re-linking the shipment.
221: func (s *dispatchService) Redispatch(ctx context.Context, tenantID uuid.UUID, req RedispatchRequest) (*RedispatchResult, error) {
222: 	// 1. Fetch the existing trip to get source data for the new trip
223: 	trip, err := s.tripSvc.GetTripByID(ctx, req.TripID)
224: 	if err != nil {
225: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("trip %s not found: %w", req.TripID, err)
226: 	}
227: 	if trip == nil {
228: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("trip %s not found", req.TripID)
229: 	}
230: 
231: 	// 2. Validate trip is in a state that can be redispatched
232: 	if trip.Status != tripModels.TripStatusCreated && trip.Status != tripModels.TripStatusScheduled {
233: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("trip must be in CREATED or SCHEDULED status to redispatch, got: %s", trip.Status)
234: 	}
235: 
236: 	// 3. Find the shipment linked to this trip
237: 	var shipmentID *uuid.UUID
238: 	var shipment *models.Shipment
239: 	if s.customerRepo != nil {
240: 		tripUUID, parseErr := uuid.Parse(req.TripID)
241: 		if parseErr == nil {
242: 			shipment, _ = s.customerRepo.GetShipmentByTripID(ctx, tripUUID)
243: 			if shipment != nil {
244: 				shipmentID = &shipment.ID
245: 				}
246: 		}
247: 	}
248: 
249: 	// 4. Check new vehicle availability
250: 	if s.dispatchRepo != nil {
251: 		activeTrips, err := s.dispatchRepo.GetActiveTripsForVehicle(ctx, req.NewVehicleID)
252: 		if err != nil {
253: 			s.logger.Warn("failed to check new vehicle availability", zap.Error(err))
254: 		} else if len(activeTrips) > 0 {
255: 			return nil, fmt.Errorf("new vehicle %s has %d active trips", req.NewVehicleID, len(activeTrips))
256: 		}
257: 	}
258: 
259: 	// 5. Fail the existing trip
260: 	if err := s.tripSvc.Fail(ctx, req.TripID, fmt.Sprintf("Redispatched: %s", req.Reason)); err != nil {
261: 		s.logger.Warn("failed to fail original trip during redispatch",
262: 			zap.String("trip_id", req.TripID), zap.Error(err))
263: 	}
264: 
265: 	// 6. Create a new trip with the same data, new vehicle/driver
266: 	tripReq, err := buildTripRequestFromExistingTrip(trip, req.NewVehicleID, req.NewDriverID)
267: 	if err != nil {
268: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to build redispatch trip request: %w", err)
269: 	}
270: 
271: 	newTrip, err := s.tripSvc.Create(ctx, tenantID.String(), tripReq)
272: 	if err != nil {
273: 		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to create new trip: %w", err)
274: 	}
275: 
276: 	// 7. Re-link shipment to the new trip
277: 	if shipment != nil {
278: 		newTripUUID, _ := uuid.Parse(newTrip.ID)
279: 		shipment.TripID = &newTripUUID
280: 		shipment.Status = "ASSIGNED"
281: 		if err := s.customerRepo.UpdateShipment(ctx, shipment); err != nil {
282: 			s.logger.Warn("failed to re-link shipment to new trip",
283: 				zap.String("trip_id", newTrip.ID),
284: 				zap.String("shipment_id", shipment.ID.String()),
285: 				zap.Error(err))
286: 		}
287: 	}
288: 
289: 	// 8. Audit: record redispatch action
290: 	if s.dispatchRepo != nil {
291: 		assignmentTx := &dispatchRepo.AssignmentTx{
292: 			TripID:     newTrip.ID,
293: 			VehicleID:  req.NewVehicleID,
294: 			DriverID:   req.NewDriverID,
295: 			TenantID:   tenantID,
296: 			ActionType: dispatchRepo.ActionRedispatch,
297: 		}
298: 		if shipmentID != nil {
299: 			assignmentTx.ShipmentID = *shipmentID
300: 		}
301: 		if _, err := s.dispatchRepo.ExecuteAssignment(ctx, assignmentTx); err != nil {
302: 			s.logger.Warn("failed to record redispatch action", zap.Error(err))
303: 		}
304: 	}
305: 
306: 	// 9. Publish events
307: 	s.publishEvent("trip.reassigned", map[string]interface{}{
308: 		"old_trip_id": req.TripID,
309: 		"new_trip_id": newTrip.ID,
310: 		"shipment_id": shipmentID,
311: 		"vehicle_id":  req.NewVehicleID.String(),
312: 		"driver_id":   req.NewDriverID.String(),
313: 		"tenant_id":   tenantID.String(),
314: 	})
315: 
316: 	s.logger.Info("trip redispatched",
317: 		zap.String("old_trip_id", req.TripID),
318: 		zap.String("new_trip_id", newTrip.ID),
319: 	)
320: 
321: 	return &RedispatchResult{
322: 		NewTripID: newTrip.ID,
323: 		Status:    "SCHEDULED",
324: 	}, nil
325: }
326: 
327: // ─── Dashboard ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
328: 
329: // GetDashboard returns a dispatch dashboard summary with real counts from the
330: // trip and customer domains. Degrades gracefully when dependencies are
331: // unavailable (e.g. in tests with nil deps).
332: func (s *dispatchService) GetDashboard(ctx context.Context, tenantID uuid.UUID) (*DashboardData, error) {
333: 	data := &DashboardData{}
334: 
335: 	if s.tripSvc != nil {
336: 		stats, err := s.tripSvc.GetStats(ctx)
337: 		if err != nil {
338: 			s.logger.Warn("failed to get trip stats for dashboard", zap.Error(err))
339: 		} else if stats != nil {
340: 			data.InProgressTrips = stats.ActiveTrips
341: 		}
342: 	}
343: 
344: 	if s.customerRepo != nil {
345: 		pending, err := s.customerRepo.CountPendingShipments(ctx, tenantID)
346: 		if err != nil {
347: 			s.logger.Warn("failed to count pending shipments for dashboard", zap.Error(err))
348: 		} else {
349: 			data.PendingShipments = pending
350: 		}
351: 	}
352: 
353: 	return data, nil
354: }
355: 
356: // ─── Event Publishing ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
357: 
358: func (s *dispatchService) publishEvent(subject string, data interface{}) {
359: 	if s.eventBus == nil {
360: 		return
361: 	}
362: 	if err := s.eventBus.Publish(subject, data); err != nil {
363: 		s.logger.Warn("failed to publish event",
364: 			zap.String("subject", subject), zap.Error(err))
365: 	}
366: }
367: 
368: // ─── Trip Request Builders ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
369: 
370: // buildTripRequestFromShipment maps a Shipment into a CreateTripRequest.
371: func buildTripRequestFromShipment(sh *models.Shipment) (*tripModels.CreateTripRequest, error) {
372: 	pickupGeo := parseLocationByte(sh.Origin)
373: 	dropGeo := parseLocationByte(sh.Destination)
374: 
375: 	waypoints := []tripModels.Waypoint{
376: 		{
377: 			Sequence: 1,
378: 			Type:     "pickup",
379: 			Location:  dereferenceLocation(pickupGeo),
380: 		},
381: 		{
382: 			Sequence: 2,
383: 			Type:     "dropoff",
384: 			Location:  dereferenceLocation(dropGeo),
385: 		},
386: 	}
387: 
388: 	var distanceKm float64
389: 	if sh.DistanceKm != nil {
390: 		distanceKm = *sh.DistanceKm
391: 	}
392: 	estimatedDuration := int(distanceKm * 2.5) // rough ETA: 2.5 min/km
393: 	if estimatedDuration == 0 {
394: 		estimatedDuration = 60
395: 	}
396: 
397: 	customerName := fmt.Sprintf("Customer %s", sh.CustomerID.String()[:8])
398: 
399: 	return &tripModels.CreateTripRequest{
400: 		Title:                fmt.Sprintf("Dispatch shipment %s", sh.ID.String()[:8]),
401: 		Description:          fmt.Sprintf("Auto-generated trip for shipment %s", sh.ID),
402: 		PlannedRoute:         waypoints,
403: 		Waypoints:            waypoints,
404: 		PlannedDistance:      distanceKm,
405: 		EstimatedDurationMin: estimatedDuration,
406: 		CargoDetails: tripModels.CargoDetails{
407: 			Description: fmt.Sprintf("Shipment %s", sh.ID),
408: 		},
409: 		ScheduledStart:    time.Now(),
410: 		CustomerName:      customerName,
411: 		CustomerPhone:     "+910000000000",
412: 		PickupGeo:         pickupGeo,
413: 		DropGeo:           dropGeo,
414: 		GSTClassification: "E",
415: 		TripType:          tripModels.TripTypeDelivery,
416: 	}, nil
417: }
418: 
419: // dereferenceLocation safely converts a *Location to a value, returning a zero
420: // Location if the pointer is nil (Waypoint.Location is a value type).
421: func dereferenceLocation(loc *tripModels.Location) tripModels.Location {
422: 	if loc == nil {
423: 		return tripModels.Location{}
424: 	}
425: 	return *loc
426: }
427: 
428: // buildTripRequestFromExistingTrip creates a CreateTripRequest from an existing
429: // trip's data, overriding only the vehicle and driver.
430: func buildTripRequestFromExistingTrip(t *tripModels.Trip, newVehicleID, newDriverID uuid.UUID) (*tripModels.CreateTripRequest, error) {
431: 	waypoints := t.Waypoints
432: 	if len(waypoints) == 0 {
433: 		waypoints = t.PlannedRoute
434: 	}
435: 	if len(waypoints) == 0 {
436: 		waypoints = []tripModels.Waypoint{
437: 			{Type: "pickup", Location: dereferenceLocation(t.PickupGeo)},
438: 			{Type: "dropoff", Location: dereferenceLocation(t.DropGeo)},
439: 		}
440: 	}
441: 
442: 	req := &tripModels.CreateTripRequest{
443: 		VehicleID:            newVehicleID.String(),
444: 		DriverID:             newDriverID.String(),
445: 		Title:                t.Title,
446: 		Description:          t.Description,
447: 		PlannedRoute:         t.PlannedRoute,
448: 		Waypoints:            waypoints,
449: 		PlannedDistance:      t.PlannedDistance,
450: 		EstimatedDurationMin: t.EstimatedDurationMin,
451: 		CargoDetails:         t.CargoDetails,
452: 		ScheduledStart:       time.Now(),
453: 		PickupGeo:            t.PickupGeo,
454: 		DropGeo:              t.DropGeo,
455: 		EWayBillNo:           t.EWayBillNo,
456: 		GSTClassification:    t.GSTClassification,
457: 		CustomerName:         t.CustomerName,
458: 		CustomerPhone:        t.CustomerPhone,
459: 		CustomerEmail:        t.CustomerEmail,
460: 		TripType:             t.TripType,
461: 	}
462: 	return req, nil
463: }
464: 
465: // parseLocationByte unmarshals a JSON byte blob from the Shipment.Origin /
466: // Destination columns into a tripModels.Location pointer.
467: func parseLocationByte(raw []byte) *tripModels.Location {
468: 	if len(raw) == 0 {
469: 		return nil
470: 	}
471: 	var loc struct {
472: 		Latitude  float64 `json:"latitude"`
473: 		Longitude float64 `json:"longitude"`
474: 		Address   string  `json:"address"`
475: 	}
476: 	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &loc); err != nil {
477: 		return nil
478: 	}
479: 	return &tripModels.Location{
480: 		Latitude:  loc.Latitude,
481: 		Longitude: loc.Longitude,
482: 		Address:   loc.Address,
483: 	}
484: }

40: 	tenantID, ok := ctxkeys.GetTenantID(r.Context())
41: 	if !ok {
42: 		http.Error(w, `{"error":"missing tenant_id"}`, http.StatusUnauthorized)
43: 		return
44: 	}

READservices/fleet-core/internal/domains/dispatch/metrics.go
Read 1/1 file

Thinking
Interesting! Looking at the actual files on disk:

1. **dispatch_service.go** (in `internal/domains/dispatch/service/`) — This is **already** a real implementation! It has:
   - `Assign()` that fetches the shipment, validates status, checks vehicle availability, builds a `CreateTripRequest` from the shipment, calls `TripService.Create()`, updates the shipment link, records audit entry, and publishes events.
   - `Redispatch()` that fetches the existing trip, validates status, finds the shipment, checks new vehicle availability, fails the old trip, creates a new trip, re-links the shipment, and publishes events.
   - `GetDashboard()` that aggregates from `TripService.GetStats()` and `CustomerRepositoryPort.CountPendingShipments()`.

2. **dispatch_handler.go** (in `internal/domains/dispatch/handlers/`) — This **already** has real routes registered: `/suggest`, `/assign`, `/redispatch`, `/dashboard`. The `Assign` and `Redispatch` handlers are real HTTP handlers that call the service. The dashboard handler has graceful degradation (returns `{"status":"ok"}` only when `dispatchSvc == nil`).

Here's what I found and did:

The dispatch domain was already significantly further along than the conversation summaries indicated. On disk, the code is far beyond the stub state described in the summaries:

  • dispatch_service.go — Already a real orchestrator. Assign() fetches the shipment, validates it's CONFIRMED, checks vehicle availability, builds a CreateTripRequest from shipment data, calls TripService.Create(), links the shipment to the trip, records an audit entry, and publishes trip.assigned/shipment.assigned events. Redispatch() fails the old trip and creates a new one. GetDashboard() aggregates real counts from TripService.GetStats() and CustomerRepository.CountPendingShipments().
  • dispatch_repository.go — Already a real pgx-backed repo with ExecuteAssignment (atomic shipment link + audit log in dispatch_actions), SaveSuggestion (into dispatch_suggestions), and GetActiveTripsForVehicle.
  • dispatch_handler.go — Already registers /dispatch/suggest, /dispatch/assign, /dispatch/redispatch, /dispatch/dashboard. Routes wired in main.go (line 468). Migration 404 already creates the dispatch_actions and dispatch_suggestions tables.

The one piece that was genuinely a stub was the auto-status worker — it was a 37-line no-op (Start() just <-ctx.Done()). I replaced it with a real Redis pub/sub consumer that subscribes to org:*:telemetry, looks up vehicle live locations (for ignition state) from the live cache, checks active trips via the trip repo, and auto-starts trips at pickup geofences (ignition ON, <50m proximity) and auto-completes at drop geofences (speed < 2 km/h). It's wired into main.go alongside the existing dispatch service, repository, and routes.

Everything builds (go build ./cmd/ passes) and all dispatch tests pass — service, handlers, integration, repository, engine, and worker (18 tests total).
